  1114	      private function qolSettingsClick(param1:MouseEvent) : void
  1115	      {
  1116	         var action:String = Sprite(param1.currentTarget).name;
  1117	         if(action == "speed")
  1118	         {
  1119	            Level.qolSpeed = Level.qolSpeed == 1 ? 3 : 1;
  1120	         }
  1121	         else if(action == "gold_add")
  1122	         {
  1123	            var amount:Number = this.qolGoldInput == null ? 0 : Number(this.qolGoldInput.text);
  1124	            if(isNaN(amount) || amount < 0)
  1125	            {
  1126	               amount = 0;
  1127	            }
  1128	            this.updateCash(int(Math.min(2000000000,amount)));
  1129	         }
  1130	         else if(action == "trees_toggle")
  1131	         {
  1132	            this.game.qolSetTreesMaxed(!this.game.qolTreesMaxed);
  1133	         }
  1134	         else if(action == "page_heroes")
  1135	         {
  1136	            this.qolSettingsPage = 2;
  1137	         }
  1138	         else if(action.indexOf("hero_") == 0)
  1139	         {
  1140	            this.qolToggleHero(action.substr(5));
  1141	         }
  1142	         else if(action == "all_waves")
  1143	         {
  1144	            this.qolSendAllWaves();
  1145	         }
  1146	         else if(action == "page_enemy")
  1147	         {
  1148	            this.qolSettingsPage = 1;
  1149	         }
  1150	         else if(action == "page_main")
  1151	         {
  1152	            this.qolSettingsPage = 0;
  1153	         }
  1154	         else if(action == "enemy_prev")
  1155	         {
  1156	            this.qolEnemyIndex = (this.qolEnemyIndex + this.qolEnemies.length - 1) % this.qolEnemies.length;
  1157	         }
  1158	         else if(action == "enemy_next")
  1159	         {
  1160	            this.qolEnemyIndex = (this.qolEnemyIndex + 1) % this.qolEnemies.length;
  1161	         }
  1162	         else if(action == "count_minus")
  1163	         {
  1164	            this.qolEnemyCount = Math.max(1,this.qolEnemyCount - 5);
  1165	         }
  1166	         else if(action == "count_plus")
  1167	         {
  1168	            this.qolEnemyCount = Math.min(200,this.qolEnemyCount + 5);
  1169	         }
  1170	         else if(action == "path_prev")
  1171	         {
  1172	            this.qolEnemyPath = Math.max(0,this.qolEnemyPath - 1);
  1173	         }
  1174	         else if(action == "path_next")
  1175	         {
  1176	            this.qolEnemyPath = Math.min(Math.max(0,this.§_-V8§.length - 1),this.qolEnemyPath + 1);
  1177	         }
  1178	         else if(action == "send_custom")
  1179	         {
  1180	            this.qolSendCustomRound();
  1181	         }
  1182	         else if(action == "hide")
  1183	         {
  1184	            this.qolHideSettings();
  1185	            return;
  1186	         }
  1187	         this.qolRenderSettings();
  1188	      }

  1218	         this.intervalWaveCounter = 0;

  1228	      private function qolMakeHero(param1:String, param2:int) : §dynamic const class§
  1229	      {
